# Drift — Task Tracker

> **Purpose:** Durable, resumable task list. This is the source of truth across sessions.
> **Created:** 2026-05-17
> **Companion to:** `master_roadmap.md`, `final_assessment.md`, `09-roadmap/execution_roadmap.md`
> **Operating contract:** All 15 rules from the project framing message apply to every task below.

---

## How to use this file

1. **Status is tracked in the checkbox** at the start of each line — never delete a task, only update its state.
2. **Stable IDs** (e.g. `QW-01`, `1.1`, `2.13`) are how we reference tasks across messages. Numeric IDs match `09-roadmap/execution_roadmap.md` for cross-reference.
3. **Sub-area headings** (`### 1.A`, `### 1.B`, …) are organisational only — they are NOT tracked tasks. Only checkbox lines are tracked.
4. **When resuming a session**, read this file first to know what's done and what's next.
5. **When a task completes**, update the checkbox here AND add a one-line completion note (commit hash / PR / file paths touched) under it.
6. **When a task is split or merged**, note it inline; never silently restructure the list.

### Status legend

| Mark | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `- [ ]` | Pending — not started |
| `- [/]` | In progress — currently being worked on |
| `- [x]` | Completed |
| `- [-]` | Skipped / superseded — keep the line, add a note explaining why |
| `- [!]` | Blocked — waiting on a decision or external input; add a note |

### Effort tags

XS = ≤ 1 hr · S = 1–4 hr · M = ½–1 day · L = 1–2 days · XL = 2–5 days

Migration **v25 `trips-soft-delete`** adds `deleted_at TEXT` to the only remaining mutable user-owned table that was missing it (trips was created in v1 and missed the v2 sweep) + partial index `idx_trips_alive ON trips(id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`. `app/src/components/Toast.js` extended with optional `opts.actionLabel` + `opts.onAction` + `opts.durationMs` — back-compat: `toast('Saved')` still works. Every `remove(id)` repo method across 9 repos now soft-deletes (`UPDATE … SET deleted_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL`) and pairs with a new `restore(id)` (`UPDATE … SET deleted_at = NULL`). The `AND deleted_at IS NULL` guard makes remove idempotent — re-firing on an already-deleted row preserves the original timestamp. **Critical**: `expenses.remove/restore/bulkRemove/bulkRestore` cascade to `receipt_items` inside a single `withTransactionAsync` because the FK ON DELETE CASCADE only fires on real DELETE; without the cascade, an expense's items would stay alive in `item_summary` after the parent soft-deletes (validated in `/tmp/drift_2_D_09_validate.mjs:84`). New repo method `expenses.attachReceiptStorage(id, {path, thumb, bytes})` UPDATEs the v7 columns in-place — leaves `receipt_uri` intact as a safety net (see Decision log). Lazy-migrate hook lives inside `ReceiptViewer.js`'s open-side `useEffect`: when `needsMigration(row)` returns true, fires `persistReceipt(row.receipt_uri)` → `expRepo.attachReceiptStorage(...)`, surfaces a "Saving permanently…" inline pill at the top while running, calls `onMigrated` to bubble the new URIs into Detail's local row-shadow state so the thumb refreshes without a provider hop. `filters.js`'s `hasReceipt` predicate flipped from `e.receipt_uri IS [NOT] NULL` to `COALESCE(e.receipt_path, e.receipt_uri) IS [NOT] NULL` so saved-filter rows match both legacy and migrated rows uniformly. 5.14 adds the predictive "Last time ₹X" chip to Add.js's merchant input. New state `lastSpend` + `chipApplied` + `lookupLastSpend(merchantId)` calling `expRepo.lastAtMerchant(merchantId)` (already added by 5.9). Two trigger points reuse the existing 5.9/5.10 wiring: `pickSuggestion(s)` (autocomplete tap — knows merchant_id directly) and `applyAliasIfAny(name, merchantId)` (free-typed merchant that resolved via bundle/user alias — chip fires when `hit.merchantId != null`). Inline `LastSpendChip` sub-component renders below the merchant input in BOTH Quick (hero card) and Detailed (MERCHANT field) layouts: F.cream pill `🕒 Last time: ₹420 · 12 May`. Tap calls `formatAmountForKeypad(lastSpend.amount)` (`X.YY` two-decimal format matching the keypad's pad-and-shift reducer) and sets `chipApplied=true` (chip flips coral with ✓); second tap clears the amount back to `'0.00'` and resets `chipApplied`. A manual keypad press cancels `chipApplied` (so a subsequent chip tap re-fills) but leaves the typed value alone. Visibility predicate: `!isIncome && !!lastSpend && (parseFloat(amount) === 0 || chipApplied)` — typing past the chip hides it, but the applied-then-edit state keeps the chip visible while the user tweaks. Merchant text changing through `onMerchantChange` clears `lastSpend` + `chipApplied` so a new merchant rehydrates from scratch. New exported helpers `formatAmountForKeypad(n)` (clamps to `'0.00'` on NaN/negative/Infinity/string/null/0; `toFixed(2)` otherwise) and `formatChipDate(iso)` (YYYY-MM-DD → "12 May"). > Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 2.A advanced again: **5.11 + 5.12** shipped in one batch. 5.11 surfaces the GST data already persisted by 4.8/4.17 as a new "Tax invoice" card on `Detail.js` (between the meta-table and the items list). Pure-JS helper `buildTaxRows(e, sym)` extracted inline in Detail.js (testable without React): renders `Subtotal · CGST(rate%) · SGST(rate%) · IGST(rate%) · Tax total · GSTIN · Invoice #` rows, each conditional on the underlying field being set. Effective-rate suffix (`(2.5%)`, `(9.0%)` etc.) computed from `cgst / subtotal * 100` and shown only when `subtotal > 0` so dodgy OCR data (amount ≤ Σtax) collapses to a clean components-only view. Subtotal row hidden when negative/zero. Tax-total row carries `emphasis: true` (cream background, weight 600) to anchor the breakdown. GSTIN row uses `fontVariant: ['tabular-nums']` + 0.5px letter-spacing for the 15-char code. No schema change, no repo change, no persistence change — every wire was already laid by 4.8 (v16 columns + Scan.js threading) and 4.17 (v17 per-item GST rates). 5.12 ships the receipt image pipeline foundation: new module `app/src/features/scan/receiptStorage.js` exports `persistReceipt(srcUri) → {path, thumb, bytes} | null`. Pipeline: lazy-requires `expo-file-system/legacy` + `expo-image-manipulator`, ensures `documentDirectory/drift/receipts/{full,thumb}/` exist (idempotent — skips makeDirectoryAsync when getInfoAsync reports exists:true), generates a UUID via `crypto.randomUUID()` with `Date.now()+Math.random()` fallback, runs `manipulateAsync(uri, [resize:{width:1600}], {compress:0.95, format:JPEG})` for full + `[resize:{width:320}], {compress:0.7}` for thumb (JPEG re-encode is the EXIF strip — manipulator constructs new bytes from decoded RGBA so the original EXIF block doesn't carry through), moves the cacheDirectory outputs into `full/<uuid>.jpg` and `thumb/<uuid>.jpg` via `moveAsync` (with `copyAsync + deleteAsync(idempotent:true)` fallback on EXDEV-style cross-volume errors). Returns `null` on any failure (require failure, manipulator throw, FS write error, missing srcUri) so the caller falls back to legacy-only save. `expenses.createWithItems` INSERT extended with `receipt_path, receipt_thumb, receipt_bytes` columns (v7 already had them — no migration needed). `Scan.js performSave` calls `persistReceipt(expense.receipt_uri)` before the DB write, merges `{receipt_path, receipt_thumb, receipt_bytes}` into the expense payload when non-null. Legacy `receipt_uri` continues to be written alongside per chosen option D — 5.15 owns the reader flip and the lazy-migrate of existing rows. `expenses.create()` and `expenses.update()` left untouched (Rule 9 — manual Add has no receipt UI; EditExpense receipt-attach is out of scope). Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **114/114 total** — `drift_5_11_detail_pure.mjs` 32/32 (15 cases: gstin-only / cgst+sgst at 2.5+2.5% / igst-only at 5% / mixed all three / amount<Σtax hides subtotal AND suppresses rates / amount=Σtax hides subtotal / invoice-only / all-zeros+empty-meta=null / whitespace-only gstin=null / null expense / 9% half-rate / USD symbol / igst-no-gstin / string-typed numerics coerced), `drift_5_12_persist_pure.mjs` 35/35 (Module._load patched to inject FS+manipulator stubs; 6 scenarios: happy path with both subdirs created + 2 manipulator calls at the right widths/quality + matching uuid full/thumb + bytes from getInfoAsync; EXDEV fallback fires copyAsync×2+deleteAsync×2 with idempotent:true; manipulator throw → null; null/empty/undefined srcUri → null; getInfoAsync missing size → bytes=0; dirs already exist → makeDirectoryAsync skipped), `drift_5_12_repo_validate.mjs` 47/47 via `node:sqlite` (applies all 24 migrations from schema.js directly; confirms expenses.{receipt_path, receipt_thumb, receipt_bytes, gstin, invoice_number, cgst, sgst, igst, payment_method} all present; INSERT with all 21 columns round-trips correctly; null-case insert preserves receipt_uri alone). > Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 2.A advanced again: **5.9 + 5.10** shipped in one batch. 5.10 ships migration v24 (`merchant-aliases`: id PK, `alias_key TEXT NOT NULL`, `merchant_id` nullable FK merchants SET NULL, `category_id` nullable FK categories SET NULL, `source` CHECK ('user'|'bundle'), `created_at/updated_at` DEFAULTS, `deleted_at` reserved for future Recycle Bin) + `UNIQUE INDEX idx_merchant_aliases_key ON (alias_key, source) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL` so a soft-deleted row can co-exist with a fresh user choice + `idx_merchant_aliases_merchant ON (merchant_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`. New bundled `app/src/features/expenses/merchantMap.json` ships **58 popular Indian merchants** (scaffold per the chosen option — full ~1k corpus deferred to a future data-fill task that won't need code review): 11 Food & Drink (Swiggy, Zomato, Domino's, McD, KFC, Starbucks, CCD), 10 Groceries (Swiggy Instamart, Blinkit, Zepto, BigBasket, DMart, Reliance Fresh/Smart, More Retail, Amul, Mother Dairy), 11 Transport (Uber, Ola, Rapido, Namma Yatri, IRCTC, IndiGo, Vistara, Air India, HP/IOC/BPCL), 7 Health (Apollo Pharmacy, MedPlus, Tata 1mg, Netmeds, PharmEasy, Cult.fit), 5 Subscriptions (Netflix, Hotstar, Prime Video, Spotify, YouTube Premium), 5 Bills (Jio, Airtel, Vi, BSNL, Tata Power), 7 Fun (Amazon, Flipkart, Myntra, AJIO, BookMyShow, PVR, INOX). All keys are pre-canonicalised via `lightNormMerchant()` (lowercased, suffix-stripped); category strings match `STARTER_CATEGORIES.name` exactly so case-insensitive lookup works against user-renamed pots too. New pure-JS module `app/src/features/expenses/aliases.repo.js` exports the `aliases` singleton with `lookup(text, {categories}) → {categoryId, merchantId?, source} | null`, `recordUserChoice({alias, merchantId, categoryId})` (UPSERT, source='user'), `clearUserAliases()` (soft-delete all user rows — reserved for a future "forget merchants" affordance), `list({source?})`, plus exported `lookupBundle(aliasKey)` for the validation harness. Two-tier lookup: user-source rows beat bundle every time; bundle's category string maps to the user's `categories` array case-insensitively, returns null if no user category matches. Substring matching via longest-key-first sorted iteration so `"Swiggy Instamart Mumbai"` correctly hits `swiggy instamart` (Groceries) before falling through to `swiggy` (Food). 5.9 extends `merchants.repo.js` with `suggest(prefix, {limit=5}) → [{id, name, canonical_name, score}]` (two-pass: exact + prefix + contains via indexed LIKE first, ranked by length asc; JW ≥ 0.78 fallback only when SQL underflows the limit) + `resolveExisting(name)` (resolve but never INSERT — used by autocomplete to attach a merchant_id without creating ghost rows). `expenses.repo.js` gains 5 new merchant-intelligence queries: `topMerchants({months, limit})` (SUM/COUNT/MAX/min group ranked by spend, gated on `e.merchant_id IS NOT NULL` and a windowed `month_key >= strftime('%Y-%m','now','-N months')` — uses the indexed `month_key`), `merchantSummary({merchantId, months})` (returns total_all/total_window/txn_count_all/avg/first_seen/last_seen in a single CASE-WHEN aggregate so the screen header reads one row), `merchantMonthlyTrend({merchantId, months})` (12-month bar chart data — joined directly against `expenses` because `monthly_summary` aggregates by category not merchant), `merchantCategoryBreakdown({merchantId, months})` (one row per category — LEFT JOIN keeps the uncategorised bucket as `id=NULL`), `merchantRecents({merchantId, limit})` (recent spends list with category join for the row UI). Plus `lastAtMerchant(merchantId)` for the future 5.14 "Last time ₹X" chip (single-row LIMIT 1 of the most-recent live row). All five queries soft-delete-aware via `NOT_DELETED_E`. `expenses.create()` now resolves `merchant_id` (was previously only `createWithItems` — manual Add path was leaving the FK NULL so MerchantDetail rollups missed quick-spend rows); `expenses.update()` re-resolves when merchant text changes (was leaving a stale FK after EditExpense merchant edits). New `MerchantDetail.js` screen mirrors `ItemTrend` structurally — hero card (name + window total + visits + avg + last_seen), 12-month tappable bar chart with selected-bar callout, category breakdown strip with per-row share bar + percentage, recent spends list tapping into `Detail`. New `Merchants.js` leaderboard with 4-range pill (3mo/6mo/12mo/All), pull-to-refresh, 1-50 rank list rendered into a single card. Both registered in `navigation/index.js` (Stack screens; MerchantDetail title threads `route.params.displayName`). `Detail.js` makes the merchant text tappable (dotted underline) only when `merchant_id` is set so legacy NULL-id rows stay non-interactive. `Profile.js` gains a "🏪 Top merchants" row in the Items & Trends section. `Add.js` rewires the merchant TextInput: `onMerchantChange` (180ms debounce) calls `merchantRepo.suggest`, renders a 5-row dropdown below the input (covers both Quick hero-card and Detailed merchant-field layouts). Tapping a suggestion fills the name + pins `pickedMerchantIdRef`; both that path and `onBlur` (free-typed merchants that didn't pick a suggestion) trigger `aliases.lookup` → silent auto-switch of `potId` ONLY when `userTouchedCategoryRef` is false (any prior pill tap permanently locks the user's manual choice for the session). Save path threads the picked merchant_id through and fires `aliases.recordUserChoice` after success so the next visit reinforces the mapping. `ExpensesProvider.addExpense/addExpenseWithItems` now return the saved row (additive — every caller was discarding the return value previously). Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **80/80 total** — `/tmp/drift_5_9_5_10_validate.mjs` 38/38 via `node:sqlite` (v1..v24 ladder; merchant_aliases column shape + UNIQUE partial-index behaviour: user/bundle coexist, duplicate-user rejected, soft-delete-then-reinsert allowed with tombstone retained; CHECK rejects bogus source; FK ON DELETE SET NULL on merchant removal; topMerchants ranking + 6/12-mo window correctness; merchantSummary CASE-WHEN sums; merchantMonthlyTrend ordering + window; merchantCategoryBreakdown including NULL category bucket; merchantRecents ordering + soft-delete exclusion + LIMIT clamp; lastAtMerchant most-recent semantics), `/tmp/drift_5_10_aliases_pure.mjs` 28/28 (bundle hit exact + Pvt-Ltd canonicalisation + longest-key substring; bundle miss; case-insensitive category mapping; user-row-wins-over-bundle + NULL-user-falls-through-to-bundle; soft-deleted-user-falls-through; lightNormMerchant edge cases; merchantMap.json hygiene — every key in canonical form + every category exists in STARTER + 58 entries + sort determinism), `/tmp/drift_5_9_suggest_pure.mjs` 14/14 against node:sqlite (exact > prefix > contains ranking; length-asc tiebreaker; JW fallback for typo "zoomato"; blank/whitespace/null safety; limit clamp; Pvt-Ltd suffix folds into canonical exact-match). Babel parse-check across all 12 touched files passes. **No user action needed** — pure SQL + JS + UI; next app launch auto-applies v24. > Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 2.C closed (apart from the explicitly-deferred 4.20): **4.24** (multi-image receipt stitching) shipped. New pure-JS module `app/src/ocr/stitchPages.js` exports `mergePages(pages)` — accepts an array of `parseReceipt()` outputs in capture order, returns a single merged parsed shape. Item dedup at fixed PRICE_EPS=0.01 by (normalized_name + |price diff|); first-occurrence wins for identity. Each output item annotated with `_pageId` (page index) + `_origIdx` (position within that page) so the Scan UI can preserve user edits across re-merges. Total reconciliation: walks pages in REVERSE, takes the first non-zero total found (last-page-grand-total wins), else falls back to sum-of-merged-items. Headers (merchant / date / gstin / orderId / invoiceNumber / format / formatLabel / brand): picked from the page with the highest `formatConfidence` whose field is non-empty (and not the parser's `Unknown store` / fallback-date sentinels). Bill-level amounts (cgst / sgst / igst / subtotal / tax): same picker, ignores zero/null values. Fees + discounts: concat (no dedup — typically unique per page). Confidence: re-scored via `scoreConfidence` on the merged shape (per-page confidences don't compose linearly). `bands` + `columns` (4.22 template capture material): last-non-null wins so the latest page's template snapshot survives. New `ScanService.scanAndProcessMore(uri, priorPages, pots)` runs the full single-page pipeline on a new image (lightPreprocess → ML Kit → optional Tesseract fallback → optional template apply per 4.21/4.22), merges with priorPages, and returns a review payload carrying `_pages`, `_pageCount`, `_parsed`, `_parsedForTemplate`. `scanAndProcess` now also attaches `_parsed` (full parsed shape) so single-page callers and the stitching path share one snapshot format. Scan.js gains `pagesRef = useRef([])` + `pageCount` state + `addingPage` in-flight flag + `addAnotherPage` / `addPageFromPicker(source)` handlers; `processImage` seeds `pagesRef = [_parsed]` and tags first-page items with `_pageId=0, _origIdx=i`; the review screen renders a new "Add page N+1 · for long receipts" pill above the Retake/Save row that prompts Camera/Gallery → runs `scanAndProcessMore` → merges with edit-preservation (`Map<pageId:origIdx, item>` lookup; user-edited rows survive; manually-added rows with no `_pageId` survive too at the end). Header preservation: merchant/date/total/taxInvoice overwritten only when current value equals the prior-merged value (skip-if-user-edited). `resetScreen` clears `pagesRef`, `pageCount`, `addingPage`. UX: single toast "Page N added" on success; Alert "Could not add page" on failure (never blocks the save flow). Validated by 2 standalone Node scripts: **78/78 total** — 41/41 `mergePages` pure-JS logic (null safety, single-page identity, 3-page overlap dedup with annotation correctness, last-page total wins, sum fallback, PRICE_EPS at boundary, header pick by formatConfidence + Unknown-store sentinel skip, GSTIN/invoice non-null win, per-component tax/subtotal picking, fees/discounts concat, format/formatConfidence max, 3-page DMart simulation 10 items + grand-total wins, bands/columns last-non-null), 37/37 structural integration (stitchPages exports, ScanService scanAndProcessMore wiring + _parsed attachment, Scan.js state declarations + handlers + first-page seeding + edit-preservation map + UI button label/disabled state + resetScreen extension). **No user action needed** — pure JS + UI, no schema, no native, no deps. Phase 2.B unchanged at 8/13; Phase 2.C now **4/5** (4.21, 4.22, 4.23, 4.24 done; only **4.20** [-] deferred); Phase 2 totals now 21/50.
> Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 2.C advanced again: **4.23** (pharmacy-specific extraction strategy) shipped. Migration v19 adds three nullable TEXT columns to `receipt_items`: `batch_no`, `expiry_date`, `mfg_date` (no indexes — no query plans on these yet; future expiry-reminder feature 7.x can add its own index). Three new keyword-anchored regexes in patterns.js: `BATCH_RE` (matches `Batch`/`B.No`/`BN`/`Lot No` + alphanumeric token incl. `/` `-`, 3..18 chars), `EXPIRY_RE` (matches `EXP`/`EXPIRY`/`Exp Dt`/`Exp.` + date), `MFG_RE` (matches `MFG`/`M.Dt`/`Mfd` + date) — all keyword-anchored so a bare date never accidentally becomes an expiry. New `extractItemsPharmacy(rows, bands)` strategy mirrors extractItemsTabular with two pharmacy-aware additions: (1) before matchAmounts, scans the current row + 3 rows back (`PHARMACY_LOOKBACK = 3`) for batch/expiry/mfg and strips the same-row matches from the working text so `B/24/011` no longer leaks `/011` into the amount list, (2) buildItem receives `batchNo`/`expiryDate`/`mfgDate` and persists them on the RawItem. New `parseDrugDate(raw, mode)` helper accepts MM/YY, MM/YYYY, DD/MM/YY, DD/MM/YYYY across `/`, `-`, `.` separators; `normaliseExpiry` uses `last`-of-month for month/year-only inputs (semantic: "valid until end of month"); `normaliseMfg` uses `first`-of-month (semantic: "manufactured at start of month"). 2-digit year window: 00..79 → 2000..2079, 80..99 → 1980..1999. `FORMAT_CONFIGS.pharmacy.itemStrategy` flipped from `tabular` → `pharmacy`; auto-activation on every receipt the existing FORMAT_SIGNATURES tag as pharmacy (Apollo Pharmacy / MedPlus / 1mg / Netmeds / PharmEasy brand hints + GSTIN). `dispatchItems` gains `case 'pharmacy'`. Both `expenses.createWithItems` and `items.replaceItems` INSERT statements extended with the 3 new columns. Out of scope: drug strength (500mg) into a dedicated column, drug form (tab/cap/syp) extraction, schedule (H/H1/X) classification, Detail-screen UI surfacing (defer to 5.11), expiry-reminder notifications (defer to 7.x). NULL persistence for non-pharmacy items), 48/48 patterns + normalisers (7 BATCH_RE positive + 4 negative; 5 EXPIRY_RE positive + 3 negative; 4 MFG_RE positive + 2 negative; 16 normaliseExpiry edge cases incl. leap-year 02/24, month/year-only last-of-month, 4-part rejection, year out-of-band; 3 normaliseMfg first-of-month; 4 two-digit year window edge cases), 30/30 structural integration (patterns exports, parseReceipt imports + helpers + PHARMACY_LOOKBACK, extractItemsPharmacy + dispatch + name-strip + buildItem extension, FORMAT_CONFIGS.pharmacy.itemStrategy flip with restaurant/departmental unaffected, both repo INSERT extensions). **No user action needed** — pure SQL + JS; next app launch auto-applies v19. New migration v18 adds `receipt_templates` (`merchant_id UNIQUE FK CASCADE`, `format`, `column_map` JSON, `header_frac`/`footer_frac` REAL with 0.15/0.20 defaults, `item_start_keyword`/`item_end_keyword`, `sample_count`, `outlier_count`, `created_at`/`updated_at`). New `app/src/features/scan/templates.repo.js` exports `templates.{getByMerchant, list, recordSample, removeByMerchant, ACTIVATION_SAMPLES=3}` + `parseColumnMap(json)`; recordSample upserts (insert on 1st sample, weighted running mean on subsequent), implements a soft outlier guard at fixed 40% relative deviation from current mean (weight 0.25 + bumps outlier_count instead of true Welford variance — minimum schema cost; see Decision log), and locks format after n>=2 to prevent late detectFormat flips. `parseReceipt(input, options={})` accepts optional `options.template`: when present, overrides format via FORMAT_CONFIGS lookup (formatConfidence floor 0.8), overrides bands via header_frac/footer_frac fractions of rangeY (gated at hf/ff < 0.5), and threads columnsOverride through dispatchItems → extractItemsColumnar (skips per-receipt detectColumns when the override has ≥ 3 columns). parseReceipt also exposes `parsed.bands = {header_frac, footer_frac}` + `parsed.columns = [{x0,x1}]` so templates.recordSample knows what to store. `ScanService.scanAndProcess` rewired: resolves merchant via merchants.resolve at scan time (one round-trip), looks up template, applies when sample_count >= 3 with the same regression-safety guard as 4.21 (only swap when templated confidence >= probe); the 4.21 Tesseract fallback now also runs with the template option so both fallback paths benefit. Attaches `processed.{merchantId, templateApplied, _parsedForTemplate}`. `expenses.createWithItems` bypasses the inner merchants.resolve when `expense.merchant_id` is already set (avoids double-resolve). `Scan.js` stashes merchant id + parsed-for-template payload in a new `templateRef`, passes merchant_id through the expense save payload, and fires `receiptTemplates.recordSample(...).catch(() => {})` after save success — same fire-and-forget discipline as the 4.19 golden capture. Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **103/103 total** — 27/27 v18 migration ladder + table structure + UNIQUE/FK CASCADE round-trip, 43/43 templates.repo behaviour (upsert, running mean, outlier weighting, format mode lock, multi-merchant independence, parseColumnMap round-trip, list/remove), 33/33 integration structure (parseReceipt template hook contract, ScanService apply/capture wiring, Scan.js merchant_id passthrough, expenses.createWithItems bypass). > Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 2.C opened with **4.21** (4.20 stays deferred). Custom Kotlin `TesseractModule` (Tesseract4Android 4.7.0 via jitpack) wraps Tesseract 5 LSTM as the Tier-2 OCR engine; the abandoned `react-native-tesseract-ocr@2.0.3` (last published 2022) was rejected as the integration path per Rule 1. New JS layer: `app/src/ocr/tesseract.js` (NativeModules wrapper with `{available: false}` sentinel for Expo Go / pre-rebuild dev), `app/src/ocr/fallback.js` (`shouldFallback(parsed, lines)` heuristic + `mergeEngineResults(mlkit, tess)` y-overlap merge with Devanagari/Tamil/Telugu/Bengali-aware swap rule). `ScanService.scanAndProcess` rewired: probe-parses ML Kit lines first, runs Tesseract only when probe-confidence is weak (lines<5, overall<0.5, items=0+total=0, or Unknown-merchant+<15 lines), merges, re-parses, and only displaces the ML Kit result when merged confidence is ≥ the prior — never a regression. `parseReceipt(ocrResultOrLines)` now accepts a pre-extracted line array so the merge path doesn't have to fabricate an ML Kit shape. `processReceipt` split into pure `buildReviewPayload(parsed, pots)` + back-compat wrapper. Native side: `TesseractModule.kt` (~140 LOC, single-threaded executor, lazy-copies assets/tessdata/*.traineddata → filesDir/tessdata-root/tessdata/ on first init, exposes `init/recognize/release` returning per-line `{text, x, y, width, height, confidence}` via Tesseract's `ResultIterator` at `RIL_TEXTLINE`); `TesseractPackage.kt` registers it manually from `MainApplication.getPackages()` (project-local, not autolinked). Gradle: `com.github.adaptech-cz.Tesseract4Android:4.7.0` added (jitpack already in root repos). ProGuard: keep rules for `com.googlecode.{tesseract,leptonica}.android.**`. Language packs (eng + hin, ~6 MB, tessdata_best) fetched via new `app/scripts/download_tessdata.sh` from `tesseract-ocr/tessdata_best` — files gitignored (whole `/android` tree already is). `golden/capture.js` extended: `processed.engine` carried through and `engine === 'mlkit+tesseract'` adds +1 to the capture-score so 4.19's dataset preferentially logs fallback-fired receipts (the most useful for measuring 4.21 efficacy when the 45-fixture corpus lands). 28/28 pure-JS validations pass (heuristic + merge: happy/low-confidence/Devanagari-swap/Latin-keep/orphan-append/partial-overlap/null-safe). **Action items for user:** (1) `cd app && bash scripts/download_tessdata.sh` to fetch eng+hin (~6 MB) before next Android build, (2) `cd app && npm run android` rebuilds the native shell — the new Kotlin module + Gradle dep require a clean install. new `ITEM_CGST_RATE_RE`/`ITEM_SGST_RATE_RE`/`ITEM_IGST_RATE_RE`/`ITEM_GST_RATE_RE` in patterns.js; new `extractItemGstRates(text)` helper in parseReceipt.js plumbs through every item-extraction strategy (card / tabular / columnar / permissive) via `buildItem({ gstRates })`; New primitive `app/src/components/primitives/PaymentPicker.js` (memoised 5-chip selector). `filters.js` un-reserves the `paymentMethods` axis: `buildWhere` emits `e.payment_method IN (…)`, `hasActiveFilters` returns true on non-empty arrays, `criteriaToHumanLabel` resolves single value via PAYMENT_LABELS (e.g. "📱 UPI") or "N payment methods" otherwise. Three new exports: `PAYMENT_METHODS` (canonical lowercase enum), `PAYMENT_LABELS` (emoji + name), `PAYMENT_EMOJI` (badge only). `FilterSheet` gains a Payment section between Mood and the footer; `togglePayment` mirrors the toggleMood pattern. `expenses.create / update / createWithItems` thread `payment_method` through the INSERT/UPDATE column lists. Add.js Expense mode renders the PaymentPicker between category and items; default seeded from the most recent expense's `payment_method`, falling back to `'upi'` on a clean install. EditExpense gains a PAYMENT section above MOOD using the same picker. Detail.js inserts a "Payment" row in the meta-table when set; AllExpenses prefixes the subline (`category · mood · recurring`) with `PAYMENT_EMOJI[pm]` when set — no layout reflow. - 2026-05-19 · 4.24 · Multi-image receipt stitching for long DMart / pharmacy receipts that don't fit a single photo (problems.md W3). New pure-JS module `app/src/ocr/stitchPages.js` exports `mergePages(pages)` taking an array of `parseReceipt()` outputs in capture order. Item dedup: same `normalized_name` + `|price diff| ≤ 0.01` (constant PRICE_EPS = 0.01). First-occurrence wins. Each merged item annotated with `_pageId` (source page index) + `_origIdx` (original position within that page) — required for the Scan UI's edit-preservation across re-merges. Helpers: `dedupItems(pages)`, `pickBestHeader(pages, field, defaultsToSkip)` (highest formatConfidence non-empty non-sentinel wins; skips `Unknown store` and fallback dates), `pickTotal(pages, mergedItems)` (walks pages in REVERSE — last-page total wins if > 0, else sum-of-merged-items), `pickAmount(pages, field)` (per-component tax / subtotal / cgst / sgst / igst — highest formatConfidence with non-zero value). Fees + discounts: concat (no dedup; typically unique per page). Confidence: `scoreConfidence` re-run on merged shape because per-page confidences don't compose linearly. `bands` + `columns` (4.22 template capture material): last-non-null wins. New `ScanService.scanAndProcessMore(uri, priorPages, pots)` exports the full single-page pipeline (lightPreprocess → ML Kit → optional Tesseract fallback per 4.21 → optional template apply per 4.22) on a new image, merges with priorPages, returns a review payload carrying `_pages` array, `_pageCount`, `_parsed` (the merged parsed shape), `_parsedForTemplate`. `scanAndProcess` extended with `processed._parsed = parsed` so single-page and stitched paths share one snapshot format. Scan.js gains 3 new state slots (pagesRef = useRef([]), pageCount, addingPage) + 2 new handlers (addAnotherPage, addPageFromPicker(source)). `processImage` seeds `pagesRef = [_parsed]` after first scan and tags every first-page item with `_pageId=0, _origIdx=i`. Review screen renders a new "📄 Add page N+1 · for long receipts" pill above the Retake/Save row (cream surface, disabled-while-in-flight with "Adding page…" + ActivityIndicator). On tap → Alert (Camera/Gallery/Cancel) → picker → scanAndProcessMore → pagesRef updated → setPageCount → edit-preservation merge (Map keyed by `pageId:origIdx`, user-edited rows kept in place; manually-added rows with no _pageId appended at end) → headers only overwritten when current value equals prior captureRef snapshot (skip-if-user-edited for merchant/date/total/taxInvoice). resetScreen clears pagesRef + pageCount + addingPage. UX: toast "Page N added" on success; Alert "Could not add page" on failure (never blocks save). Validated by 2 standalone Node scripts: **78/78 total** — `drift_stitch_pages_validate.mjs` 41/41 (null safety, single-page identity with _pageId tagging, 2-page merge with overlap correctness across 7 sub-assertions, last-page total wins, sum-of-items fallback when no page has total, PRICE_EPS boundary at 0.005 vs 0.02, highest-formatConfidence header pick, Unknown-store sentinel skip, GSTIN / invoice number non-null wins, per-component amounts via formatConfidence, fees/discounts concat = sum-of-pages, confidence re-scored, format mode + formatConfidence max, 3-page DMart simulation 10 unique items with grand-total wins, bands/columns last-non-null), `drift_stitch_integration_validate.mjs` 37/37 (stitchPages exports + structure + helpers, ScanService imports + scanAndProcessMore wiring + _parsed attachment, Scan.js imports + 3 state declarations + 2 handler declarations + first-page seeding + edit-preservation Map + UI button label "Add page N+1" + disabled-while-in-flight + resetScreen extensions clearing pagesRef + pageCount). **No user action needed** — pure JS + UI; no schema, no native, no deps. Phase 2.C now 4/5 (4.21, 4.22, 4.23, 4.24 done; 4.20 [-] deferred) · uncommitted · `app/src/ocr/stitchPages.js` (new, ~170 LOC), `app/src/features/scan/ScanService.js:1-10,131-138,140-180` (mergePages import + processed._parsed attach + scanAndProcessMore export), `app/src/features/scan/screens/Scan.js:8,52-58,108-115,144-243,294-296` (scanAndProcessMore import + multi-page state + processImage seeding + addAnotherPage/addPageFromPicker handlers + resetScreen extensions + "Add page" UI pill in review stage), `/tmp/drift_stitch_{pages,integration}_validate.mjs` (2 harnesses, not committed)
- 2026-05-19 · 4.23 · Pharmacy-specific item extraction. Migration v19 (`pharmacy-item-metadata`) appends 3 nullable TEXT columns to `receipt_items`: `batch_no`, `expiry_date`, `mfg_date`. Patterns: new `BATCH_RE` / `EXPIRY_RE` / `MFG_RE` in `app/src/ocr/patterns.js`, each keyword-anchored (Batch/B.No/BN/Lot · EXP/EXPIRY/Exp Dt · MFG/M.Dt/Mfd) so bare dates and random brand strings never produce false positives. Capture group 1 holds the value. Helpers in `parseReceipt.js`: `parseDrugDate(raw, mode)` parses MM/YY|MM/YYYY|DD/MM/YY|DD/MM/YYYY across `/`, `-`, `.` separators (plausibility window 1980..2099; 2-digit year window 00..79→2000s, 80..99→1900s); `normaliseExpiry`/`normaliseMfg` thin wrappers picking last-of-month vs first-of-month respectively; `extractPharmacyMeta(rows, idx, lookback=3)` scans current row + 3 rows back for batch/expiry/mfg, returning the matched values plus an array of [start,end] strip ranges for same-row hits only. New strategy `extractItemsPharmacy(rows, bands)` mirrors `extractItemsTabular` with two pharmacy-aware additions: (1) strips batch/expiry/mfg ranges from the working text BEFORE matchAmounts() so `B/24/011` no longer feeds `/011` into the amount list (root cause of the "batch numbers corrupt prices" gap from problems.md:399), (2) passes batchNo/expiryDate/mfgDate to buildItem. `buildItem` extended with three new optional fields, all defaulting to null. `FORMAT_CONFIGS.pharmacy.itemStrategy` flipped from `tabular` → `pharmacy` in detectFormat.js (restaurant/departmental unaffected). dispatchItems gains `case 'pharmacy': return extractItemsPharmacy(rows, bands)`. Persistence: both `expenses.createWithItems` and `items.replaceItems` INSERT statements extended (column list + bind values). Drug strength (e.g. "500mg"), drug form (tab/cap), schedule (H/H1/X), and Detail-screen UI surfacing are explicitly out of scope this batch — flagged in Decision log. Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **97/97 total** — `drift_v19_validate.mjs` 19/19 (migration ladder v1→v19, column structure + nullability + defaults, INSERT round-trip incl. NULL persistence for non-pharmacy items), `drift_pharmacy_patterns_validate.mjs` 48/48 (BATCH_RE 7 positives + 4 negatives; EXPIRY_RE 5 positives + 3 negatives; MFG_RE 4 positives + 2 negatives; normaliseExpiry 16 edge cases incl. leap-year 02/24→2024-02-29, month/year-only → last-of-month, 4-part rejection, year-out-of-band rejection; normaliseMfg 3 cases; 2-digit year window 4 edge cases at 79/80/99/00 boundaries), `drift_extractItemsPharmacy_validate.mjs` 30/30 (patterns exports, parseReceipt imports + helpers, PHARMACY_LOOKBACK=3 constant, extractPharmacyMeta strip-range tracking, dispatch case wiring, name-strip-before-matchAmounts, buildItem extension, FORMAT_CONFIGS.pharmacy flip + restaurant/departmental unaffected, both repo INSERTs) · uncommitted · `app/src/db/schema.js:643-656,762-766` (V19_SQL + migration), `app/src/ocr/patterns.js:102-128` (BATCH_RE / EXPIRY_RE / MFG_RE), `app/src/ocr/parseReceipt.js:31-33,388-409,411-503,608-685,759` (imports + buildItem extension + pharmacy helpers + extractItemsPharmacy + dispatch case), `app/src/ocr/detectFormat.js:57-67` (itemStrategy flip), `app/src/features/expenses/repo.js:165-194` (INSERT extension), `app/src/features/items/repo.js:172-201` (INSERT extension), `/tmp/drift_{v19,pharmacy_patterns,extractItemsPharmacy}_validate.mjs` (3 harnesses, not committed)
- 2026-05-19 · 4.22 · Per-merchant template learning loop. New migration v18 (`receipt-templates`) creates `receipt_templates` (id PK, merchant_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES merchants(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, format TEXT NOT NULL, column_map TEXT JSON, header_frac REAL DEFAULT 0.15, footer_frac REAL DEFAULT 0.20, item_start_keyword TEXT, item_end_keyword TEXT, sample_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0, outlier_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0, created_at/updated_at TEXT DEFAULTS datetime('now')); `TABLES` array gets receipt_templates inserted between expenses-side rollups and merchants (child-first FK order for resetAll). New `app/src/features/scan/templates.repo.js` exports `templates` object: `getByMerchant(merchantId) → row|null`, `list()`, `removeByMerchant(merchantId)`, `ACTIVATION_SAMPLES = 3`, `recordSample({merchantId, parsed})` (insert on first sample with parsed bands/format/columns; subsequent samples weighted-mean header/footer_frac using `(mean*n + value*w)/(n+w)`, with 40% relative-deviation triggering outlier weight 0.25 + bumping outlier_count; format mode locks to existing after n>=2 so a late detectFormat misclassification can't flip a stable learned format; column_map carries forward from latest non-outlier sample) + `parseColumnMap(json)` helper returns null on any parse failure so callers can silently fall back to per-receipt detectColumns. `parseReceipt(ocrResultOrLines, options = {})` accepts second arg; when `options.template` present: format swapped via `FORMAT_CONFIGS[template.format]` (formatConfidence floor 0.8), bands swapped via `itemBandTop = minY + rangeY*header_frac`, `itemBandBottom = minY + rangeY*(1-footer_frac)` (gated at 0 <= frac < 0.5), columnsOverride threaded through dispatchItems → extractItemsColumnar (used when override has ≥ COLUMNAR_MIN_COLS columns, skipping per-receipt detectColumns). Parser also exposes `parsed.bands = {header_frac, footer_frac}` (rounded to 3 decimals) and `parsed.columns = [{x0,x1}]` so the capture path knows what to store. `ScanService.scanAndProcess` rewired with the apply path between probe-parse and the 4.21 Tesseract fallback: resolves merchant via `merchants.resolve(parsed.merchant)` (one extra round-trip — may INSERT a new merchants row if first scan of merchant, idempotent with save-time resolve), looks up template, and when `sample_count >= templates.ACTIVATION_SAMPLES` re-parses with template; only swaps when templated `confidence.overall >= probe` (same regression-safety as 4.21). The 4.21 fallback path now also passes the template option so both branches benefit. Attaches `processed.{merchantId, templateApplied, _parsedForTemplate}` to the review payload — last is internal-only (underscore-prefixed) for the capture call. `expenses.createWithItems` extended with a one-line bypass: `expense.merchant_id != null ? expense.merchant_id : await merchants.resolve(expense.merchant)` so a scan-time-resolved id flows through the transaction without a second JW match. `Scan.js` gains a `templateRef = useRef({merchantId, parsedForTemplate})` parallel to the 4.19 captureRef, plumbs `merchant_id` into `buildSavePayload`'s expense object, and fires `receiptTemplates.recordSample({merchantId, parsed: parsedForTemplate}).catch(() => {})` after a successful save — same fire-and-forget discipline as the 4.19 golden capture. - 2026-05-19 · 4.21 · Tesseract LSTM fallback OCR — three-tier stack closed at Tier 2. New JS: `app/src/ocr/fallback.js` (`shouldFallback(parsed, lines)` triggers on lines<5 OR overall<0.5 OR (items=0 AND total<=0) OR (Unknown-merchant AND <15 lines); `mergeEngineResults(mlkit, tess)` does y-overlap pairing with ≥0.5 ratio, swaps in Tesseract text when it carries non-Latin glyphs and ML Kit version is pure-Latin (Devanagari/Tamil/Telugu/Bengali blocks tested), keeps ML Kit frame even on swap, appends Tesseract-only orphans, sorts top-to-bottom-left-to-right). `app/src/ocr/tesseract.js` — `TesseractEngine.{available,init,recognize}` wraps `NativeModules.Tesseract` with `{available:false, lines:[]}` sentinel on Expo Go / web / iOS / pre-rebuild so the fallback gracefully skips when the native module isn't linked. `parseReceipt(ocrResultOrLines)` accepts either an ML Kit result or a pre-extracted line array (one-line `Array.isArray` short-circuit at the orchestrator top — required by the merge path). `processReceipt` split into pure `buildReviewPayload(parsed, pots)` + back-compat wrapper. `ScanService.scanAndProcess` orchestrates: lightPreprocess → mlKit → extractLines → probe parseReceipt → if shouldFallback: TesseractEngine.recognize → mergeEngineResults → reparseReceipt → only swap when merged confidence ≥ prior (regression-safety); attaches `parsed.engine = 'mlkit' | 'mlkit+tesseract'` and propagates to `processed.engine`. `golden/capture.js` extended: `processed.engine === 'mlkit+tesseract'` adds +1 to capture-score and writes the `engine` field into the candidate payload so the 4.19 corpus can quantify fallback efficacy once it grows. Native: `app/android/app/src/main/java/com/drift/expensemanager/TesseractModule.kt` (140 LOC, single-threaded executor, lazy assets→filesDir copy on first init, `RIL_TEXTLINE` iterator returns text+bbox+confidence/100) + `TesseractPackage.kt` + `MainApplication.kt` registers `TesseractPackage()`. New `app/src/features/expenses/aliases.repo.js` (~130 LOC) — two-tier lookup: SELECT user-source WHERE alias_key=? AND deleted_at IS NULL first; on miss, longest-key-first substring search of the bundle JSON, then case-insensitive name match against `categories` to derive a usable category_id (returns null if no live category matches). `recordUserChoice` does SELECT-then-UPDATE/INSERT against the partial-unique index, refreshing `updated_at` so the most-recent choice wins. `clearUserAliases` reserved for a future "forget merchants" affordance. `merchants.repo.js` extends with `suggest(prefix, {limit})` — SQL pass (exact + prefix + contains with rank CASE-WHEN ordered by length asc) cap-3× the limit then JW≥0.78 fallback only when SQL underflows + `resolveExisting(name)` that does the resolve()-style match without ever inserting (safer for the autocomplete path). `expenses.repo.js` extends with 5 merchant queries + `lastAtMerchant`: `topMerchants` (SUM/COUNT/MAX, joined-merchants-only, indexed `month_key` window, ordered total DESC ties → count DESC → last_seen DESC), `merchantSummary` (single-row CASE-WHEN aggregation: total_all/total_window/txn_count_all/avg/first_seen/last_seen), `merchantMonthlyTrend` (12 months via month_key — joined directly against expenses since monthly_summary is category-bucketed, not merchant-bucketed), `merchantCategoryBreakdown` (LEFT JOIN preserves NULL-category bucket as `id=null`), `merchantRecents` (Detail-row shape with category join, soft-delete-aware, LIMIT clamp). `expenses.create()` re-resolves `merchant_id` so manual Add path stops leaving the FK NULL; `expenses.update()` re-resolves only when the merchant text changes (avoids spurious resolution on amount-only edits). New `app/src/features/expenses/screens/MerchantDetail.js` mirrors `ItemTrend`: hero card with name + window total + visits + avg + last_seen, 12-month tappable bar chart with selected-bar callout in F.cream, per-category breakdown with horizontal share bar + percentage label, recent spends list tapping into Detail. New `app/src/features/expenses/screens/Merchants.js` leaderboard with 4-range pill (3mo/6mo/12mo/All=600mo proxy), pull-to-refresh, ranked 1..50 list, empty state for cold-start. Save threads the picked merchant_id through and fires `aliases.recordUserChoice` fire-and-forget after success so the user's latest decision overrides the bundle next time. `ExpensesProvider.addExpense/addExpenseWithItems` now return the saved row (additive — every caller was previously discarding the return value). - 2026-05-20 · 2.D.05 · Mood picker is now optional on Add + EditExpense, and Scan no longer silently stamps 😌. `MoodPicker` primitive extended with `selected: boolean` (default true → back-compat) + `onClear?: () => void`: when `selected=false` it renders a single self-aligning ＋ Add mood pill (F.surface bg, F.line border, F.ink2 label, accessibilityLabel="Add mood"); when `selected=true` it renders the existing 5-emoji row + label, plus a top-right Clear link whenever onClear is supplied. `Add.js` splits the old `[mood, setMood] = useState(1)` into `[moodIdx, setMoodIdx] = useState(1)` + `[moodOn, setMoodOn] = useState(false)`. The Mood card body now wires `selected={moodOn}` + `onChange={(i) => { setMoodIdx(i); setMoodOn(true); }}` (first face tap selects + engages) + `onClear={moodOn ? () => setMoodOn(false) : undefined}` (Clear only when engaged so the chip-state doesn't show a vestigial link). Both quick-mode and detailed-mode save paths write `mood: moodOn ? MOODS[moodIdx] : null` — fresh expenses with no mood touch now store NULL instead of the legacy silent 😌. `EditExpense.js` applies the same dual-state with `moodOn = !!e?.mood` seeded from the existing row, so moded rows open expanded with their stored face selected (Clear visible) and unmoded rows open as the chip. Both update callsites flipped to `mood: moodOn ? MOODS[moodIdx] : null`; the legacy `initialMoodIdx = e?.mood ? MOODS.indexOf(e.mood) : 1` fallback line is intentionally untouched (Step 2 Q4) — it still produces a sensible index when the picker is expanded, and `moodOn` independently gates whether that index reaches the DB. `Scan.js` L318 hard-coded `mood: '😌'` flipped to `mood: null` so OCR-saved expenses no longer get a silent stamp; no UI added to Scan review (would be a new affordance, deferred). **No schema migration** — `expenses.mood TEXT` was already nullable at v1 and `expRepo.create` already coerces `undefined → null` (`mood ?? null` at repo.js L81). Static verification across every reader confirmed null-safe rendering: `Home.js:299` and `AllExpenses.js:339` use truthy-guards, `Detail.js:120` + `PotDetail.js:127` wrap in `e.mood && (...)`, `filters.js:157` gates the IN-list on `c.moods.length > 0`, `export.js:59` uses `e.mood || ''`. No tests in this repo and no headless dev runtime — UI flow needs manual verification on emulator (`cd app && npm start && npm run android`): Add quick+detailed both default to chip; tap chip → faces appear with 😌 highlighted; tap face → selection moves + chip latches engaged; Clear returns to chip; save without engaging → Detail omits mood block. EditExpense on a legacy moded row → opens expanded with stored face; Clear → save → mood gone. Scan flow → save without touching anything → Detail has no mood. Phase 2 totals 36/50. Overall **114/187 = 60.96%** · uncommitted · `app/src/components/primitives/MoodPicker.js` (rewritten, +25 LOC), `app/src/features/expenses/screens/Add.js:69-70, 259, 290, 491-501`, `app/src/features/expenses/screens/EditExpense.js:23-25, 66, 79, 166-176`, `app/src/features/scan/screens/Scan.js:318`
- 2026-05-19 · 5.13 + 5.14 + 5.15 · Receipt viewer + predictive chip + reader flip + lazy migrate shipped in one batch. 5.13: new `app/src/features/expenses/components/ReceiptViewer.js` — full-screen `Modal` (transparent, rgba(0,0,0,0.92) backdrop, animationType='fade') hosting a `GestureDetector` over `Animated.View` from RNGH 2.28 + Reanimated 4.1 (both already installed). Gestures composed via `Gesture.Simultaneous(pinch, Gesture.Race(doubleTap, pan))`: pinch bounded `[1, 4]` with snap-back to 1× when below 1.05; pan only active when scale > 1.05, deltas clamped against `(scale-1) * dim / 2` so the image can't leave the canvas; double-tap toggles 1× ↔ 2×. All RNGH + Reanimated imports lazy-required so a dev shell missing the native rebuild falls through to a static contain-fit Image. Detail.js renders a new "Receipt" card between the Tax-invoice card and the Items list when `e.receipt_path || e.receipt_uri` is set — 64×88 thumb (cover fit, F.cream placeholder when no thumb) + "Tap to view · Pinch to zoom · drag to pan" hint + chevron; tap opens the viewer Modal. 5.15: new shared pure-JS module `app/src/features/expenses/receiptUri.js` exports `pickReceiptUri(e) → {full, thumb}` (prefers permanent path/thumb, falls back to legacy uri), `hasReceipt(e)`, `needsMigration(e)`. New repo method `expenses.attachReceiptStorage(id, {path, thumb, bytes})` UPDATEs the v7 columns in-place — leaves `receipt_uri` intact (safety net if future expo upgrades shift documentDirectory). Lazy-migrate hook lives in ReceiptViewer.js's open-side effect: when `needsMigration(row)` returns true, fires `persistReceipt(row.receipt_uri)` → `expRepo.attachReceiptStorage(...)`, surfaces a "Saving permanently…" inline pill while running, calls `onMigrated` to bubble the new URIs into Detail's local row-shadow state so the thumb refreshes without a provider hop. `filters.js` `hasReceipt` predicate flipped from `e.receipt_uri IS [NOT] NULL` to `COALESCE(e.receipt_path, e.receipt_uri) IS [NOT] NULL` so saved-filter rows match both legacy + migrated rows uniformly. 5.14: Add.js gains `lastSpend` + `chipApplied` state + `lookupLastSpend(merchantId)` that fires `expRepo.lastAtMerchant(merchantId)` (already added by 5.9). Two trigger points: `pickSuggestion(s)` (autocomplete tap, knows the id directly) and `applyAliasIfAny(name, merchantId)` (free-typed merchant that resolved through bundle/user alias). Inline `LastSpendChip` sub-component renders below the merchant input in BOTH Quick + Detailed layouts: F.cream pill `🕒 Last time: ₹420 · 12 May`. Tap calls `formatAmountForKeypad(lastSpend.amount).toFixed(2)` and sets `chipApplied=true` (chip flips to coral + ✓); second tap clears amount back to `'0.00'` and resets `chipApplied`. Manual keypad press cancels `chipApplied` (so a subsequent tap on the chip re-fills). Visibility predicate: `!isIncome && !!lastSpend && (parseFloat(amount) === 0 || chipApplied)` — so typing past the chip hides it until cleared, but the applied-then-edit state keeps it visible while the user adjusts. Merchant text changing through onMerchantChange clears `lastSpend` + `chipApplied` so a new merchant rehydrates from scratch. New helpers `formatAmountForKeypad(n)` (clamps to '0.00' on NaN/negative/Infinity/string; `toFixed(2)` otherwise — matches the keypad's two-decimal pad-and-shift state) and `formatChipDate(iso)` (YYYY-MM-DD → "12 May") exported for testability. Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **63/63 total** — `/tmp/drift_5_13_pickreceipt_pure.mjs` 21/21 (all 8 (path/thumb/uri) combinations + null safety + hasReceipt incl. thumb-only=false + needsMigration including empty-uri=false), `/tmp/drift_5_14_chip_pure.mjs` 28/28 (formatAmountForKeypad across 11 inputs incl. Infinity/NaN/null/negative/string; formatChipDate across 8 incl. malformed; tap-state machine 4-step trace incl. keypad-cancel; visibility predicate 5 cases incl. income mode + applied-then-edit), `/tmp/drift_5_15_migrate_validate.mjs` 14/14 via `node:sqlite` (4-row corpus: legacy/migrated/no-receipt/soft-deleted-legacy; pre-migrate hasReceipt=true matches legacy+migrated, hasReceipt=false matches no-receipt, soft-delete excluded; attachReceiptStorage stamps path/thumb/bytes while retaining receipt_uri; idempotent re-attach overwrites; null-path no-op; null-thumb permitted; null/missing id no-op). Babel parse-check across all 6 touched files passes via `@babel/parser`. **No user action needed** — pure JS + UI, no schema change (v7 already covered the columns), no new dependencies (RNGH + Reanimated already installed), no native rebuild. Phase 2.A now 14/15 (5.2–5.15 all done; only 5.1 remains as marked done-in-Phase-3). Phase 2 totals 35/50 · uncommitted · `app/src/features/expenses/components/ReceiptViewer.js` (new), `app/src/features/expenses/receiptUri.js` (new), `app/src/features/expenses/screens/Detail.js:1-7, 60-66, 143-172, 240-249`, `app/src/features/expenses/screens/Add.js:9-10, 18-33, 73-95, 152-173, 175-191, 326-330, 358-364, 487-510`, `app/src/features/expenses/repo.js:131-148`, `app/src/features/expenses/filters.js:24, 153-156`, `/tmp/drift_5_13_pickreceipt_pure.mjs` + `/tmp/drift_5_14_chip_pure.mjs` + `/tmp/drift_5_15_migrate_validate.mjs` (harnesses, not committed)

New `app/src/ocr/golden/runner.js` exports `runGolden({fixtures, processReceipt}) → {total, passed, failed, results}` with tolerant compares (case-insensitive trimmed merchant, ±0.01 amount, first-10-chars date) and per-fixture field-level diffs. New `app/src/ocr/golden/capture.js` exports: `assessForCapture(processed) → {score, reasons}` (signals: low/medium confidence label, needsReview flag, low formatConfidence, generic format, no-items-with-total — threshold 2); `writeCandidate({ocr, processed, saved})` writes a JSON to `documentDirectory/drift/golden-candidates/<ts>.json` when score ≥ 2 OR user edited any of merchant/date/total/items (edit detection adds +3 to score); `bundleForExport()` zips every candidate into a single JSON at `cacheDirectory/drift-golden-export.json`; `getEnabled/setEnabled` toggle backed by `.config.json` in the candidates dir (no DB migration); `listCandidates/clearCandidates`; rotation caps at 200, evicting oldest unedited first. ## Decision log

Record any binding decisions taken during execution (per Rule 8 — explain impact). Format:

```
- 2026-MM-DD · <decision> · <rationale> · <impact>
```

- 2026-05-17 · QW-01 scope kept minimal (try/finally only; no `db.closeAsync()` on schema error, no inline error logging) · Rule 9 — keep refactors tight; defensive close is opportunistic and `logError` helper is task 1.9 · Impact: if `execAsync(SCHEMA)` throws after `openDatabaseAsync` succeeds, the opened connection is dropped and GC'd by expo-sqlite rather than explicitly closed. No leak; minor untidiness on retry. Reconsider if a real-world failure shows up.
- 2026-05-17 · QW-02 took the `crypto.randomUUID()` + fallback path rather than literal `useRef` · The shorthand "→ useRef" in quick_wins.md cannot be applied to module-level exported `emptyRow()` / `rowsFromExisting()` without changing their signatures and updating 3+ external callers (scope creep for an XS task). The deeper recommendation in architecture_recommendations.md Improvement 17 actually proposes the random/UUID approach · Impact: keys now globally unique across hot-reloads and parallel screen mounts; no external callers affected.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-03 non-produce branch routed via `parsed.format` rather than "first non-grocery pot" or narrower fixes · Leverages existing format-detection investment (`detectFormat.js`) and matches the 11 declared formats; produce-heavy override preserves the original grocer-first instinct for any-format receipts · Impact: receipts now land in the correct category on first scan for fuel, pharmacy, restaurant, food-delivery, departmental, quick-commerce, utility, online-retail; generic/handwritten still fall back to `pots[0]`. If user renames default categories, matcher misses silently and falls back — better than the prior always-grocery behavior.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-04 PNG re-encode deferred to task 4.11 (`lightPreprocess`) rather than installing `expo-image-manipulator` now · Avoids Rule-1 dep additions in an XS quick-win; 4.11 will add resize + CLAHE + deskew + PNG in one place · Impact: only the `quality: 1.0` half of the spec lands today. Picked images are uncompressed but remain in source format (JPEG from camera, mixed from gallery). 4.11 closes the PNG gap.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-04 limited to `Scan.js` only; Add.js / EditExpense.js untouched · Those screens have no `ImagePicker` calls today — the tracker listed them anticipatorily. Wiring receipt-image attach into Add/EditExpense is real scope (storage, save-path) and belongs in task 5.12 (receipt image pipeline) · Impact: no change to Add/EditExpense UX; QW-04 closes cleanly with the two call sites that exist.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-05 included `wal_autocheckpoint = 1000` even though it isn't in the QW-05 one-liner · The dual-referenced task 1.3 lists all six PRAGMAs; splitting the single PRAGMA block across two edits would have been churn · Impact: closes QW-05 and 1.3 together. WAL files now self-checkpoint every 1000 pages — bounds sidecar growth and avoids the unbounded-WAL pathology on long sessions.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-07 took the direct-manifest-edit path instead of also adding `android.blockedPermissions` to `app.json` · User selected the simpler option after seeing both tradeoffs; `app.json` already lists only the 3 desired perms so prebuild was not the leak source — the extras were inherited from older library manifest versions at the original prebuild run · Impact: if `expo prebuild --clean` is ever re-run from current deps, the three removed perms should not regenerate; if it ever DOES re-add them, follow up with `blockedPermissions`.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-08 noted `expo-camera` dep + plugin remains in app.json/package.json despite zero source-level use · QW-08 / 1.13 spec asked to "confirm expo-camera need", not remove it. Removing it is its own task because (a) the plugin block contributes manifest entries other deps may rely on, (b) Rule 9 — don't bundle unrelated removals · Impact: ~2 MB bundle bloat still present; flag as a follow-up cleanup task before the production cut.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-09 kept `\p{N}` (numbers) in the normalized name despite the upstream regex spec saying "letters+whitespace" · The class also catches digits that survive SKU/qty stripping (e.g. brand names like "7up" or "5 Star"); `singularise()` is Latin-only so non-Latin scripts are untouched by the suffix pass; NFC done before lowercase to avoid casing inconsistencies on combining marks · Impact: brand-name digits no longer collapsed to whitespace; combined NFC + Unicode class recovers the entire Devanagari/Tamil receipt corpus.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-10 kept the babel `@core` alias work for later (task 2.1) · Relative `../core/domain/...` paths are slightly longer to read but require zero tooling changes; 2.1 will sweep them when it lands · Impact: no Babel/Metro reconfiguration risk in this batch; the alias swap will be a future find-replace pass.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-10 also left old `idx_expenses_category` in place alongside the new `idx_exp_cat_date` per the user-selected QW-11 option · See the related QW-11 decision; documenting here so a future "redundant index audit" task knows both decisions landed together.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-11 kept old `idx_expenses_category` despite being subsumed by the new composite under leftmost-prefix · User-selected option; the per-insert overhead is ~1 page; explicit DROP belongs in a dedicated cleanup task with full query-plan verification · Impact: writes carry a tiny redundant index cost until the cleanup task; reads are unaffected (SQLite picks the better index).
- 2026-05-18 · QW-12 chose a custom Reanimated banner over `ToastAndroid` · Cross-platform consistency, themeable via the same `F` palette every screen uses, and the host serves as the substrate for future Phase-2 UX items (2.D.07 swipe-to-delete, 2.D.09 undo snackbar). The `ToastProvider` sits inside `AppProvider` so it reads theme; outside `Navigation` so it floats above every screen · Impact: ~80 lines of new component code; no new deps. Banner uses `pointerEvents="none"` so it never intercepts taps. Re-firing replaces the prior toast in-place.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-18 scope kept tight: helper + only the two confirmed silent-swallow sites · The "replace empty catch {} across screens" framing in `quick_wins.md` implied broader sweep, but a `grep` across `app/src` found exactly one true `catch {}` (`Home.js:26`) and one near-empty (`ItemRows.js:121` swallowed `setSuggestions([])`). Every other try/catch in the app already has a real body (mostly `Alert.alert`). Avoided churning files that don't need changes per Rule 9 · Impact: developer-only signal in Metro; release builds tree-shake the `__DEV__` branch. Future task 1.9-style sweeps (when surface grows) can plug into the same helper.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-19 also moved `tabBar={(props) => <CustomTabBar {...props}/>}` into a module-constant `renderTabBar` · Wrapping `CustomTabBar` in `React.memo` while the parent re-creates the wrapping arrow each render is functionally pointless — the wrapper changes identity even when state/F don't. Module-constant fixes that and matches React-Navigation's documented "stable function reference" recommendation. The remaining concern — `CustomTabBar`'s `useApp()` call still subscribes the bar to every theme/state mutation — is by design; the memo only avoids re-render when nothing in `state` or `useApp()` changed · Impact: no behavioural change; perf gain measurable only with profiler. The constant declaration moved below `CustomTabBar` so reading the file top-to-bottom is straightforward.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-20 ErrorBoundary uses a static fallback palette (not `F` from `useApp()`) · The boundary must continue working if `AppProvider` itself crashed (e.g. DB open failure), so it cannot read context. Hard-coded the Flow cream/coral/ink tokens directly into the fallback view. Class component (not hook) because React 19 still requires `componentDidCatch` to live on a class · Impact: fallback UI looks correct in light mode but doesn't theme-switch in dark mode. Acceptable trade-off for crash-time resilience. Dev builds also print `componentStack` to Metro for diagnosis.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-20 per-screen boundaries applied via `withBoundary(name, Comp)` HOC at module top of `navigation/index.js`, not by editing each screen file · Keeps the screen files free of boundary-awareness, makes the wrapping audit trivial (one file lists every boundary), and avoids `displayName`/StrictMode surprises that would come from inline `component={withBoundary('X', X)}` per route (creates a new component identity each render → screen tree remounts on every nav re-render) · Impact: 20 screens now isolated; a crash inside Trends shows the fallback there while Home keeps working. The outer `<ErrorBoundary name="Drift">` in App.js catches anything outside `Navigation` (typically AppProvider boot errors).
- 2026-05-18 · QW-17 derived last-used category from in-memory `expenses[0].category_id` rather than persisting a settings key · Zero schema change, zero new write path, and reflects ACTUAL most-recent usage even after edits/deletes — a settings-table mirror would diverge whenever the user edits or deletes their last expense. Validates category still exists in current `pots` before defaulting (handles the "deleted my last category" edge case), then falls back to `pots[0]` · Impact: first session for a brand-new user defaults to `pots[0]` (no expenses yet) — same as before. Cost: a single `useMemo` on `[expenses, pots]` runs on Add mount; negligible.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-15 removed the header right "Save" link entirely (replaced with a 48px spacer for symmetry) instead of leaving both header and bottom · Two save affordances would compete for attention; quick_wins.md item #18 says "primary action gets primary visual weight". Bottom CTA in both modes now shows the live amount (`Save · ₹X.XX`) so the user gets running feedback that the header link never offered. Quick-mode keypad's safe-area `paddingBottom` collapsed since the CTA bar below it now owns the bottom inset · Impact: single source of truth for save; CTA always visible above keyboard since it sits below the keypad and the keypad is the height-fixed sibling of the ScrollView.
- 2026-05-18 · QW-14 Add removed from Tab.Screen list entirely (not kept as a placeholder route) · The cleaner alternative was a stub Tab.Screen that auto-navigates to the modal stack, but that leaves a phantom route in `state.routes` that we'd then need to skip in the tab bar — circular. Better: synthesise the `+` button as a static cell at index 2 of the visual row, sandwiched between `state.routes[1]` (Scan) and `state.routes[2]` (Trends). `navigation.getParent()?.navigate('Add')` is the standard React-Navigation pattern for tab→stack hops · Impact: tabs are now Home/Scan/[+]/Trends/Subs (5 visual cells, 4 actual routes + 1 static). The Tab.Navigator initial route is unchanged (Home). Saving in Add still calls `navigation.goBack()`, which now pops the modal back to whatever tab the user came from (previously: switched to the Add tab).
- 2026-05-18 · QW-13 chose `@react-native-community/datetimepicker` over a custom JS picker · Standard Expo-supported native module, ~zero maintenance, opens the OS-native date dialog (already familiar to users). Display mode forked: `'inline'` on iOS (recommended by the package), `'default'` (modal-spinner) on Android. Date stored as `YYYY-MM-DD` string for compatibility with existing parser output and `expenses.expense_date` schema. `maximumDate=new Date()` prevents future-dated receipts. Requires an `expo prebuild` re-run for the native module to link · Impact: one new native dep; if the user later swaps to managed Expo, the package is auto-supported. No app.json plugin block needed for prebuild workflow.
- 2026-05-18 · 1.1 / 1.2 legacy-stamp path used instead of forcing every existing user through v1.up · The current schema is a fully idempotent `CREATE IF NOT EXISTS` blob, but re-running it on a populated DB would land an `applied_at = now` row that lies about the install age. Probing `sqlite_master` for all 10 v1 tables (`V1_REQUIRED_TABLES`) before stamping handles partial-install corner cases — if even one table is missing, we let v1.up run and its `IF NOT EXISTS` guards prevent any conflict · Impact: user's existing install on `Drift.apk` boots into v1 with zero DDL re-run, audit trail honest. Phase 1.C (3.1+) lands as v2..v14 on top of the stamp.
- 2026-05-18 · 1.1 / 1.2 `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON` lifted out of the v1.up DDL blob into the runtime PRAGMA block in `db/index.js` · `foreign_keys` is a per-connection setting (resets to OFF on every new connection) — not DDL. Leaving it inside v1.up meant it ran exactly once at v1 install and never again; the `expo-sqlite` connection probably re-enabled it via its own defaults, but the contract was wrong. Now it's set on every `openDatabaseAsync`, before any migration runs · Impact: every connection now actually enforces FKs (especially `expenses.category_id → categories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` and `receipt_items.expense_id → expenses(id) ON DELETE CASCADE`). No behavioural regression if expo-sqlite was already setting it; defence-in-depth if it wasn't.
- 2026-05-18 · 1.6 sort + cap helper (`sortExpenses`) used after every patch instead of always prepending · The SQL `ORDER BY expense_date DESC, created_at DESC, id DESC` is the canonical order; a back-dated expense (scanned today, dated last week) prepended would visibly jump to the top of AllExpenses until next refresh — a bug visible to the user. Sorting + slicing back to `EXPENSES_LIMIT=500` on every patch keeps the in-memory array byte-identical to what `expRepo.list({ limit: 500 })` would return · Impact: O(n log n) on a 500-row array per mutation — sub-ms on any device. The cap stays at 500 until 8.1 ships FlatList virtualisation, then it can drop.
- 2026-05-18 · 1.7 `pots` shape preserved via in-memory `rowToPot` mapper rather than touching the 8 consumer screens · `summaryByCategory` returns `spent` (SQL aggregate convention), and screens read `pot.spend` / `pot.key` / `pot.label`. Mapping at the boundary in `useAppState` keeps the diff to one file and respects Rule 9 (no churn beyond the task). The canonical shape (drop the aliases, use `spent` everywhere) becomes a follow-up cleanup task once any other Phase-1 work is touching those screens · Impact: zero screen changes, identical render output, identical rounding (`+(spent\|\|0).toFixed(2)` matches the previous `+(byCat.get(...)\|\|0).toFixed(2)`).
- 2026-05-18 · 1.6 / 1.7 every category mutation now also calls `refreshSummary()` even though categories aren't part of 1.6's scope · `pots` are derived from `categories` joined to expense aggregates; adding a category, changing a budget, or renaming/recoloring one all change the pots payload. Without the refresh, `Home`/`Trends` would stay stale until the user pulled-to-refresh. Cost is one tiny aggregate query · Impact: category list and pots stay in lockstep. Removing a category still leaves stale `category_id` on in-memory expenses (FK SET NULL is async-from-the-client's-pov); the next `expRepo.list()` reload (refresh or app restart) repairs it. Acceptable — the orphan rows don't show in pots anyway.
- 2026-05-18 · 1.10 keystore stored at `~/.drift/drift-release.jks` (outside the repo) and credentials at `app/android/keystore.properties` (inside the gitignored `/android` tree) — not in `~/.gradle/gradle.properties` as the execution_roadmap.md:233 risk note literally suggested · `~/.gradle/gradle.properties` is process-wide for the user and any other Android project would inherit `storeFile` / `keyAlias` keys, which is the inverse of "secrets that belong to this app". `keystore.properties` adjacent to `build.gradle` is the documented React Native pattern, sits behind two layers of .gitignore (the project's `/android` rule and a defensive explicit `keystore.properties` line), and uses `chmod 600` for filesystem-level isolation. Functionally equivalent for credential safety; ergonomically much better for a solo-developer single-app repo · Impact: a fresh clone with no `keystore.properties` produces a debug-signed release APK + a WARNING line at Gradle config time. That's a friendly default for local dev but means CI/release pipelines must explicitly verify the WARNING is absent before publishing.
- 2026-05-18 · 1.10 keystore is PKCS12 (not JKS) · keytool emits a literal "It is recommended to migrate to PKCS12" warning on JKS creation as of JDK 21; PKCS12 is the industry standard cross-platform format and Gradle reads both interchangeably. Regenerated as PKCS12 from the start rather than running the post-hoc `keytool -importkeystore` migration · Impact: zero behavioural difference for signing; keystore is portable to any toolchain (apksigner, jarsigner, OpenSSL) without conversion.
- 2026-05-18 · 1.10 release build falls back to **debug** signing (not failure) when `keystore.properties` is missing · A hard-fail would block local dev for anyone who clones the repo without the secret. Soft-fall + loud WARNING preserves the QW-08-era ability to run `assembleRelease` for size-checking on any clean clone. The trade-off: a release pipeline that ignores the WARNING could ship a debug-signed APK; documented in `android-signing.md` as the explicit pre-publish check · Impact: net-positive for solo dev workflow; release-pipeline hygiene now relies on an apksigner verify step (also documented).
- 2026-05-18 · 1.11 `shrinkResources` left disabled even though R8 minify is on · Resource shrinking can drop drawables/strings that Reanimated or RN reflectively load (image-picker placeholders, font assets, etc.), and Drift currently has zero UI regression coverage. Once 2.D.* polish tasks land + we have visual snapshot or device-walkthrough automation, flip `android.enableShrinkResourcesInReleaseBuilds=true` and measure the additional savings · Impact: ~1-3 MB of potential APK savings deferred; safety net intact.
- 2026-05-18 · 1.11 proguard-rules.pro covers every dep currently on the classpath, including ones (`expo-camera`) the source no longer uses · Removing keep rules for soon-to-be-evicted deps would couple this task to QW-08's "confirm expo-camera need" follow-up. Cleaner: keep them now, drop them in the same PR that removes the dep. No size or correctness cost (R8 only removes classes that are unreferenced AND lack keep rules; keeping a rule for an absent class is a no-op) · Impact: zero. The keep set is a superset of what's strictly needed and survives any single dep removal without re-edit.
- 2026-05-18 · 1.11 release-APK size dropped 143MB → 138MB (3.5%) with minify on, not the 30%+ commonly cited for R8 · Drift's bytecode payload is small (one screen-set, no analytics SDKs, no Firebase, etc.) — the bulk is native libs across 4 ABIs (Hermes ~25MB×4, ML Kit text recognition models ~12MB, expo-sqlite native ~4MB, Reanimated turbomodule ~3MB×4). R8 only shrinks Java/Kotlin code, and that's the small slice of the pie · Impact: the bigger size wins are 8.6 (WebP receipt thumbnails) and ABI splits (split APKs per arch, deferring 3 of the 4 ABIs from each APK). Note this so we don't chase R8-as-a-size-tool further in Phase 1.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.1 only adds the resolver plugin; the actual import sweep (`../core/...` → `@core/...`) intentionally deferred · QW-10's decision log already flagged the sweep as a separate find-replace pass. Doing it inline would have been ~30 files of churn for zero behavioural change; Babel's resolver works for both styles simultaneously so the swap can happen incrementally as files are touched · Impact: aliases are usable in new code immediately; existing relative paths keep working. Metro cache must be cleared on the first dev start after this lands (`npx expo start --clear`) since Babel transform output is cached.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.5 `src/data/` directory removed entirely · `constants.js` was its only resident and the three single-export files now live in `core/domain/`. Future bundled JSON (4.2 `product_synonyms.json`, `hindi_product_map.json`) will land in `core/domain/` alongside the matching matcher logic, not in `data/`. One less top-level src/ folder; cleaner mental model · Impact: nothing imports from `../data/` any more; ripgrep clean post-removal.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.6 split into both `daysUntil(d) → number` and `daysUntilLabel(d) → string` instead of merging or just lifting one signature · Home.js's old `daysUntil` did both the math AND the label formatting; Travel.js's old `daysUntil` did just the math and inlined its own labels at the call site. Lifting only the label version would force Travel to parse string outputs (broken), and lifting only the numeric version would force Home to repeat the four-case ternary at its call site. Two-layer primitive matches how the two screens actually want to consume it — Home wants a label, Travel wants raw days · Impact: zero behavioural change at either call site; `daysUntilLabel` is a thin shim (~6 lines) and only one screen calls it today, so the second export costs nothing.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.7 `ThemeProvider` nested INSIDE `AppContext.Provider` (not above it) · The dark-mode boolean lives inside AppProvider's own state (`settings.dark_mode`), so ThemeProvider can't sit above it without re-implementing the settings read. Nesting it inside means `useTheme()` only works for screens that already live under `AppProvider` — but every screen does, so this is a non-restriction. `useApp().F` was deliberately kept exposed too (Rule 9): swapping all 19 screens to `useTheme()` belongs in a separate sweep and would mix scope with 2.9's per-feature provider split · Impact: zero churn outside navigation/. Future task to delete `F` from AppContext value can ripgrep `useApp().F` and migrate one screen at a time without breaking older callers mid-sweep.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.7 `useTheme()` memoises on `[dark]` rather than returning a fresh object every render · Returning `{ F, dark }` literally each render would invalidate every `useTheme()`-consumer's render even when `dark` didn't change. The memo only re-creates the value object when the boolean flips (which happens once per user-initiated theme toggle, never during normal usage). Mirror of how `useAppState`'s `value = {...}` works · Impact: navigation tree no longer re-renders on every state change in AppProvider — only on dark-mode flip. Tiny but real win once more consumers adopt the hook.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.8 `CustomTabBar` consumes `useTheme()` directly, not `useApp()` · The tab bar only needs `F`; it had no other reason to subscribe to AppContext. Switching to `useTheme()` decouples the bar from all the non-theme state in AppContext (expenses, categories, etc.), so a mutation in those slices no longer re-renders the bar. The existing `React.memo` wrapper from QW-19 + module-constant `renderTabBar` now actually pays off — previously even with the memo, the bar re-rendered whenever AppContext's value object identity changed (i.e. on every mutation) · Impact: tab bar re-renders only on theme toggle and `state.index` change. Sets the template for migrating other AppContext-only-for-F components in future cleanup.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.11 chose the roadmap-literal taxonomy with `home` / `trends` / `scan` as standalone features (not folded into `items` or `analytics`) · Matches the 8-feature list 2.9 names verbatim + 3 dashboard/orchestration features that own one screen each. Anticipating Phase 3 (analytics expansion) without pre-creating an `analytics/` folder that would force a re-shuffle when 6.12-6.22 land — Trends will likely merge with an analytics hub then; today it's its own feature, tomorrow's redesign can collapse · Impact: 11 feature folders today, possibly 10 after Phase 3 lands. Folder names mirror UX surfaces, not domain models.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.11 swept moved screens to `@core` / `@components` / `@ocr` aliases instead of the longer `'../../../core/...'` relative paths · The earlier 2.1 / QW-10 decisions deferred a global alias sweep ("don't sweep files we're not touching"), but since 2.11 was rewriting every import in all 21 moved files anyway, using aliases there is in-scope and saves a future cleanup pass. Files NOT moved (components/, db/, hooks/, navigation/) keep their existing relative paths · Impact: the moved files read cleaner; the rest of the tree is untouched and will migrate when those files are next edited.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.11 `'../../../hooks/useAppState'` kept relative (not aliased) · No `@hooks` alias exists in babel.config.js and adding one is technically out of scope. The relative path will become a non-issue if/when `useApp()` is deleted (every screen migrates to direct `useExpenses()` etc.) or moves into `@core/state/`. Same reasoning for `'../../../theme'` which has no alias either · Impact: cosmetic. Two import lines per screen file remain longer than the rest.
- 2026-05-18 · 2.12 `settings.js` repo placed at `features/profile/settings.repo.js` (filename suffix, not its own folder) · Settings has no screens of its own — it's per-user app config (currency, dark_mode, carbon_tracking) configured FROM the Profile screen. Giving it its own feature folder would be a one-file folder; co-locating with profile reflects how the UX flow already groups them. The `.repo.js` suffix prevents collision with the new `profile/context.js` and `profile/settings.context.js` files alongside it · Impact: a follow-up could promote settings to `core/state/settings/` if it ever grows screens of its own (settings page, configurator).
